		public static PromptType[] VAPromptInit()

		private static void ParseDataFromReactDist(ref SentCopy.SENTGROUP sg, string modelType)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(sg.name, CompatibleReactDist.sourceName, TextCompare: false) == 0) & (Operators.CompareString(sg.modeltype, CompatibleReactDist.sourceModel, TextCompare: false) == 0) & (Operators.CompareString(modelType, CompatibleReactDist.chemModel, TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				sg.name = CompatibleReactDist.chemName;
				sg.modeltype = CompatibleReactDist.chemModel;
			}
		}

		private static void CopyOneIdSentence(ref IHNodeCol iNodeCol, ref SentCopy.SENTGROUP sg, int rxn, string senType)
		{
			string[] keyNode = new string[2] { "DELT", "S_D_KBASIS" };
			string[] keyNode2 = new string[9] { "A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "HREACTION", "TREF", "KREF", "STOIC_OPTION" };
			string[] keyNode3 = new string[2] { "K_DELT", "CALC_KEQ" };
			string[] keyNode4 = new string[9] { "K_A", "K_B", "K_C", "K_D", "K_E", "HFUSION", "TMELT", "DELTA_CP", "SALT_OPTION" };
			Collection Collector = new Collection();
			bool flag = false;
			switch (senType)
			{
			case "STOIC-DATA":
				ExtractKeysFromNode(ref Collector, keyNode, iNodeCol, rxn);
				break;
			case "K-STOIC":
				ExtractKeysFromNode(ref Collector, keyNode2, iNodeCol, rxn);
				break;
			case "SALT-DATA":
				flag = true;
				ExtractKeysFromNode(ref Collector, keyNode3, iNodeCol, rxn);
				break;
			case "K-SALT":
				flag = true;
				ExtractKeysFromNode(ref Collector, keyNode4, iNodeCol, rxn);
				break;
			}
			if (Collector.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			IHNodeCol elements = iNodeCol[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Collector[1]), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].Elements;
			if (elements[0, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)] != null)
			{
				int num;
				if (flag)
				{
					string id = iNodeCol[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Collector[1]), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].Elements[rxn, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].get_Name(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value));
					num = SentCopy.SGAddSentence(ref sg, senType, id);
				}
				else
				{
					num = SentCopy.SGAddSentence(ref sg, senType, rxn);
				}
				int count = Collector.Count;
				for (int i = 1; i <= count; i = checked(i + 1))
				{
					ref SentCopy.SGSENTENCE sent = ref sg.sentences[num];
					string name = Conversions.ToString(Collector[i]);
					IHNode node = iNodeCol[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Collector[i]), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)];
					int num2 = SentCopy.SGAddVariable(ref sent, ref name, ref node);
				}
			}
		}

		public static void PasteSentence(ref IHNodeCol inputCol, ref SentCopy.SGSENTENCE sent, string sVar)
		{
			IHNodeCol elements = inputCol[sVar, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].Elements;
			if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectGreater(sent.nids, 1, TextCompare: false))
			{
				string text = Conversions.ToString(sent.id[2]);
				IHNodeCol elements2 = elements[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(sent.id[1]), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].Elements;
				if (elements2.get_LabelLocation(text, 0) == -1)
				{
					elements2.set_Label(0, elements2.get_RowCount(0), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), text);
					sent.id[2] = text;
				}
				elements2 = null;
			}
			int nvars = sent.nvars;
			for (int i = 1; i <= nvars; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				string name = sent.variables[i].name;
				if (Operators.CompareString(name, sVar, TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					ref SentCopy.SGVARIABLE var = ref sent.variables[i];
					IHNode node = inputCol[sVar, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)];
					SentCopy.SGStoreVariable(ref sent, ref var, ref node);
				}
			}
		}

		public static void PasteSentences(ref IHNodeCol inputCol, ref SentCopy.SENTGROUP sg, int insrow, string sSent, string sVar)
		{
			IHNodeCol nodeCol = inputCol[sVar, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].Elements;
			int count = nodeCol.Count;
			checked
			{
				string[] array = new string[count - 1 + 1];
				bool[] array2 = new bool[count - 1 + 1];
				int num = 0;
				if (count > 0)
				{
					num = OverrideDialog(ref nodeCol, ref sg, sSent);
					int num2 = count - 1;
					for (int i = 0; i <= num2; i++)
					{

						array2[i] = true;
					}
				}
				bool flag = false;

				for (int i = 1; i <= nsents; i++)
				{
					flag = false;
					ref SentCopy.SGSENTENCE reference = ref sg.sentences[i];
					if (Operators.CompareString(reference.name, sSent, TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectGreater(reference.nids, 1, TextCompare: false))
						{
							int num3 = count - 1;
							for (int j = 0; j <= num3; j++)
							{
								if (!Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(array[j], reference.id[1], TextCompare: false))
								{
									continue;
								}
								if ((num == 6) & array2[j])
								{
									IHNodeCol elements = nodeCol[RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(reference.id[1]), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].Elements;
									array2[j] = false;
									int num4 = elements.get_RowCount(0);
									for (int k = num4; k >= 1; k += -1)
									{
										elements.RemoveRow(0, k - 1);
									}
									elements = null;
								}

								{
									flag = true;

								sg.sentences[i].id[2] = text;
							}
							elements2 = null;
						}

						for (int j = 1; j <= nvars; j++)
						{
							string name = reference.variables[j].name;
							if (Operators.CompareString(name, sVar, TextCompare: false) == 0)
							{

					{

					}

		public static int OverrideDialog(ref IHNodeCol nodeCol, ref SentCopy.SENTGROUP sg, string sSent)
		{
			string text = "Reactant of Salt";
			if (Operators.CompareString(sg.name, "CHEMISTRY", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				text = "Reactant of Salt or Dissociation";
			}
			string text2 = text + " is already existed. Do you want to override?";
			int num = 0;
			int count = nodeCol.Count;
			int nsents = sg.nsents;
			checked
			{
				for (int i = 1; i <= nsents; i++)
				{
					ref SentCopy.SGSENTENCE reference = ref sg.sentences[i];
					if (Operators.CompareString(reference.name, sSent, TextCompare: false) != 0 || !Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectGreater(reference.nids, 1, TextCompare: false))
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num2 = count - 1;
					for (int j = 0; j <= num2; j++)
					{
						unchecked
						{
							if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(nodeCol[j, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].get_Name(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)), reference.id[1], TextCompare: false))
							{
								num = (int)MessageBox.Show(text2, "Chemistry", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
								if (num == 6 || num == 7)
								{
									goto end_IL_013a;
								}
							}
						}
					}
					continue;
					end_IL_013a:
					break;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		private static bool ExtractKeysFromNode(ref Collection Collector, string[] KeyNode, IHNodeCol iNode, int rn)
		{
			bool result = false;
			checked
			{
				int num = KeyNode.Length - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
				{
					string text = Conversions.ToString(iNode[KeyNode[i], RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].Elements[rn, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)].get_Value(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)));
					if (text != null)
					{
						Collector.Add(KeyNode[i]);
						result = true;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		private void cmdCancel_Click(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			int try0001_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					string text;
					switch (try0001_dispatch)
					{
					default:
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						text = "";
						TPSARMod.m_CancelPaste = true;
						Close();
						break;
					case 80:
						num = -1;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 2:
							text = Information.Err().Description;
							break;
						default:
							goto end_IL_0001;
						}
						break;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(text, "", TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						Interaction.MsgBox(text, MsgBoxStyle.OkOnly, "Aspen Plus");
					}
					break;
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0001_dispatch = 80;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void cmdGenerate_Click(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			int try0001_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					checked
					{
						string text;
						switch (try0001_dispatch)
						{
						default:
						{
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
							num2 = 2;
							int num3 = 0;
							int num4 = m_numExistingSecID - 1;
							for (int i = 0; i <= num4; i++)
							{
								if (m_arrExistingSecID[i] > num3)
								{
									num3 = m_arrExistingSecID[i];
								}
							}
							AxMSHFlexGrid axMSHFlexGrid = mhfSecID;
							int num5 = axMSHFlexGrid.Rows - 1;
							for (int i = 1; i <= num5; i++)
							{
								int num6 = Conversions.ToInteger(axMSHFlexGrid.get_TextMatrix(i, 2));
								if (num6 > num3)
								{
									num3 = num6;
								}
							}
							axMSHFlexGrid = null;
							text = TPSARMod.m_XMLTPSARSections.GenerateNewID(num3);
							if (Operators.CompareString(text, "", TextCompare: false) == 0)
							{
								BindSecIDs();
							}
							break;
						}
						case 254:
							num = -1;
							switch (num2)
							{
							case 2:
								text = Information.Err().Description;
								break;
							default:
								goto end_IL_0001;
							}
							break;
						}
						if (Operators.CompareString(text, "", TextCompare: false) != 0)
						{
							Interaction.MsgBox(text, MsgBoxStyle.OkOnly, "Aspen Plus");
						}
						else
						{
							Interaction.MsgBox("New section IDs have been successfully generated to avoid conflicts with existing section IDs.", MsgBoxStyle.OkOnly, "Aspen Plus");
						}
						break;
					}
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0001_dispatch = 254;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

			txtEdit.Visible = false;

		private void frmSecIDs_Load(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			int try0001_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					string text;
					switch (try0001_dispatch)
					{
					default:
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						text = "";
						m_IgnoreTxtEditChange = false;
						m_CurrentCol = -1;
						m_CurrentRow = -1;
						mstTheTab.TabPages[0].Text = "New " + ObjectCopyUtilsMod.GetTPSARTypeName(TPSARMod.m_TPSARType) + " Section IDs";
						mstTheTab.TabPages[1].Text = "Existing " + ObjectCopyUtilsMod.GetTPSARTypeName(TPSARMod.m_TPSARType) + " Section IDs";
						fraSecID.Text = "New " + ObjectCopyUtilsMod.GetTPSARTypeName(TPSARMod.m_TPSARType) + " section IDs";
						fraExistingSecID.Text = "Existing " + ObjectCopyUtilsMod.GetTPSARTypeName(TPSARMod.m_TPSARType) + " section IDs";
						BindSecIDs();
						BindExistingSecIDs();
						m_ConflictAmongNewID = false;
						m_ConflictWithExistingID = false;
						optIgnore.Checked = true;
						base.KeyPreview = true;
						break;
					case 307:
						num = -1;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 2:
							text = Information.Err().Description;
							break;
						default:
							goto end_IL_0001;
						}
						break;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(text, "", TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						Interaction.MsgBox(text, MsgBoxStyle.OkOnly, "Aspen Plus");
					}
					break;
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0001_dispatch = 307;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void frmSecIDs_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.KeyCode == Keys.F1)
			{
				cmdHelp_Click(this, EventArgs.Empty);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				e.Handled = false;

		private void CtrlAppearance()
		{
			int try0001_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					checked
					{
						string text;
						switch (try0001_dispatch)
						{
						default:
						{
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
							num2 = 2;
							text = "";
							bool conflictWithExistingID = false;
							bool conflictAmongNewID = false;
							AxMSHFlexGrid axMSHFlexGrid = mhfSecID;
							bool[] array = new bool[axMSHFlexGrid.Rows - 1 + 1];
							int num3 = axMSHFlexGrid.Rows - 1;
							for (int i = 1; i <= num3; i++)
							{
								array[i] = false;
							}
							int num4 = axMSHFlexGrid.Rows - 1;
							for (int i = 1; i <= num4; i++)
							{
								int num5 = Conversions.ToInteger(axMSHFlexGrid.get_TextMatrix(i, 2));
								if (ObjectCopyUtilsMod.IDExists(num5, m_numExistingSecID, ref m_arrExistingSecID))
								{
									array[i] = true;
									conflictWithExistingID = true;
								}
								TPSARMod.m_XMLTPSARSections.SetConflictFlag(Conversions.ToInteger(axMSHFlexGrid.get_TextMatrix(i, 1)), array[i]);
								int num6 = i - 1;
								for (int j = 1; j <= num6; j++)
								{
									int num7 = Conversions.ToInteger(axMSHFlexGrid.get_TextMatrix(j, 2));
									if (num7 == num5)
									{
										array[j] = true;
										array[i] = true;
										conflictAmongNewID = true;
									}
								}
							}
							axMSHFlexGrid.Col = 2;
							int num8 = axMSHFlexGrid.Rows - 1;
							for (int i = 1; i <= num8; i++)
							{
								axMSHFlexGrid.Row = i;
								if (array[i])
								{
									axMSHFlexGrid.CellBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromOle(255);
								}
								else
								{
									axMSHFlexGrid.CellBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromOle(-2147483643);
								}
								if ((m_CurrentCol == axMSHFlexGrid.Col) & (m_CurrentRow == axMSHFlexGrid.Row))
								{
									txtEdit.BackColor = axMSHFlexGrid.CellBackColor;
								}
							}
							axMSHFlexGrid = null;
							m_ConflictWithExistingID = conflictWithExistingID;
							m_ConflictAmongNewID = conflictAmongNewID;
							cmdGenerate.Enabled = m_ConflictWithExistingID;
							fraOption.Enabled = m_ConflictWithExistingID;
							optIgnore.Enabled = m_ConflictWithExistingID;
							optOverride.Enabled = m_ConflictWithExistingID;
							break;
						}
						case 575:
							num = -1;
							switch (num2)
							{
							case 2:
								text = Information.Err().Description;
								break;
							default:
								goto end_IL_0001;
							}
							break;
						}
						if (Operators.CompareString(text, "", TextCompare: false) != 0)
						{

						}

		private void txtEdit_TextChanged(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			int try0001_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			string left = default(string);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					string text;
					switch (try0001_dispatch)
					{
					default:
					{
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						text = "";
						left = "";
						if (m_IgnoreTxtEditChange || m_CurrentCol != 2 || m_CurrentRow <= 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						int iXMLID = Conversions.ToInteger(mhfSecID.get_TextMatrix(m_CurrentRow, 1));
						left = mhfSecID.get_TextMatrix(m_CurrentRow, m_CurrentCol);
						string text2 = Strings.Trim(txtEdit.Text);
						if (Operators.CompareString(text2, "", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							text = "New section ID must be specified.";
							break;
						}
						if (!Versioned.IsNumeric(text2))
						{
							text = "New section ID must be an integer";
							break;
						}
						int num3 = Conversions.ToInteger(text2);
						if (num3 < 1 || num3 > 999)
						{
							text = "New section ID must be greater than zero and less than 1000.";
							break;

		private double PixelsToTwipsY(double pixels)
		{
			object right = graph.DpiY;
			return Conversions.ToDouble(Operators.DivideObject(pixels * 1440.0, right));
		}

		private double PixelsToTwipsX(double pixels)
		{
			object right = graph.DpiX;
			return Conversions.ToDouble(Operators.DivideObject(pixels * 1440.0, right));
		}

		private double TwipsToPixelsY(double twips)
		{
			object right = graph.DpiY;
			return Conversions.ToDouble(Operators.DivideObject(Operators.MultiplyObject(twips, right), 1440.0));
		}

		private double TwipsToPixelsX(double twips)
		{
			object right = graph.DpiX;
			return Conversions.ToDouble(Operators.DivideObject(Operators.MultiplyObject(twips, right), 1440.0));
		}
	}

		private static void PasreDissToEquil(ref SentCopy.SENTGROUP sgDiss)
		{
			SentCopy.SENTGROUP sg = default(SentCopy.SENTGROUP);
			SGCreateEquil(ref sg);
			checked
			{

		}

		private static void ParseDataFromChemistry(ref SentCopy.SENTGROUP sg, string modeltype)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(sg.name, CompatibleChemistry.sourceName, TextCompare: false) == 0) & (Operators.CompareString(sg.modeltype, CompatibleChemistry.sourceModel, TextCompare: false) == 0) & (Operators.CompareString(modeltype, CompatibleChemistry.reactModel, TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				sg.name = CompatibleChemistry.reactName;
				sg.modeltype = CompatibleChemistry.reactModel;
				if (Operators.CompareString(sg.sentences[1].name, CompatibleChemistry.dissType, TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					PasreDissToEquil(ref sg);
				}
			}
		}

		{

		public static string CreateNewVariableName(string name, string suffix)
		{
			if (name.Length < 8)
			{
				return name + suffix;
			}
			return name.Substring(0, checked(8 - suffix.Length)) + suffix;
		}

	[StandardModule]
	internal sealed class SentCopy
	{
		[Serializable]
		public struct SGVARIABLE
		{
			public string name;

			public object value;

			public string units;

			public object subvalues;
		}

		[Serializable]
		public struct SGSENTENCE
		{
			public string name;

			public object nids;

			public object[] id;

			public short nvars;

			public SGVARIABLE[] variables;
		}

		[Serializable]
		public struct SENTGROUP
		{
			public int version;

			public string name;

			public string modeltype;

			public short nsents;

			public SGSENTENCE[] sentences;
		}

		private static void ReadVariable(ref SGVARIABLE var, ref byte[] b)
		{
			MemoryStream serializationStream = new MemoryStream(b);
			BinaryFormatter binaryFormatter = new BinaryFormatter();
			object obj = binaryFormatter.Deserialize(serializationStream);
			var = ((obj != null) ? ((SGVARIABLE)obj) : default(SGVARIABLE));
		}

		private static byte[] WriteVariable(ref SGVARIABLE var)
		{
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryFormatter binaryFormatter = new BinaryFormatter();
			binaryFormatter.Serialize(memoryStream, var);
			return memoryStream.ToArray();
		}

		private static byte[] WriteSentence(ref SGSENTENCE sent)
		{
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryFormatter binaryFormatter = new BinaryFormatter();
			binaryFormatter.Serialize(memoryStream, sent);
			return memoryStream.ToArray();
		}

			object obj = binaryFormatter.Deserialize(serializationStream);
			sent = ((obj != null) ? ((SGSENTENCE)obj) : default(SGSENTENCE));
		}

		{

			binaryFormatter.Serialize(memoryStream, sg);
			return memoryStream.ToArray();
		}

		private static bool SGCompatible(string currentSentence, string clipSentence)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (Operators.CompareString(currentSentence, "REACTDIST", TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				if (Operators.CompareString(currentSentence, "CHEMISTRY", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					if (Operators.CompareString(clipSentence, "REACTDIST", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = true;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (Operators.CompareString(clipSentence, "CHEMISTRY", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static int CanCopyTrayVariables(IHNode xNode, string sVarName, int npass, string swType)
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (xNode.get_Value(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value)) != null)
			{
				if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_NPASS", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					result = 1;
				}
				if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_DC_WTOP_S", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					result = 1;
				}
				if ((npass == 2 || npass == 4) && Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_DC_WTOP_C", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					result = 1;
				}
				if (npass >= 3)
				{
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_DC_WTOP_O", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_DC_OFC_LC", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
				}
				if (Operators.CompareString(swType, "PKWEIR", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_PIC_SIDE", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
					if ((npass == 2 || npass == 4) && Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_PIC_CTR", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
					if (npass >= 3)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_PIC_OFCIN", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							result = 1;
						}
						if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_PIC_OFCOU", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							result = 1;
						}
					}
				}
				if (Operators.CompareString(swType, "SBWEIR", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_SW_S", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_SW_A", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_SW_B", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_SW_SF", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(sVarName, "CA_SW_WF", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = 1;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

	[StandardModule]
	internal sealed class ObjectCopyUtilsMod
	{
		public static string GetTPSARTypeName(TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum iTPSARType)
		{
			string result = "";
			switch (iTPSARType)
			{
			case TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.PackRating:
				result = "Pack Rating";
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.PackSizing:
				result = "Pack Sizing";
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.TrayRating:
				result = "Tray Rating";
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.TraySizing:
				result = "Tray Sizing";
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.CAOption:
				result = "CAOption";
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.CASection:
				result = "CASection";
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR:
				result = "NotTPSAR";
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string GetTPSARTypeNameWOBlank(TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum iTPSARType)
		{
			string tPSARTypeName = GetTPSARTypeName(iTPSARType);
			return Strings.Replace(tPSARTypeName, " ", "");
		}

		public static TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum GetTPSARTypeFromName(string sTPSARType)
		{
			TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR;
			switch (sTPSARType)
			{
			case "Pack Rating":
			case "PackRating":
				result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.PackRating;
				break;
			case "Pack Sizing":
			case "PackSizing":
				result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.PackSizing;
				break;
			case "Tray Rating":
			case "TrayRating":
				result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.TrayRating;
				break;
			case "Tray Sizing":
			case "TraySizing":
				result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.TraySizing;
				break;
			case "Column Internal":
			case "CAOption":
				result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.CAOption;
				break;
			case "Sections":
			case "CASection":
				result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.CASection;
				break;
			case "NotTPSAR":
				result = TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool IsTPSAR()
		{
			if (TPSARMod.m_TPSARType == TPSARCls.TPSARTypeEnum.NotTPSAR)
			{
				bool flag = false;
			}
			else
			{
				bool flag = true;
			}
			bool result = default(bool);
			return result;
		}

	internal class TPSARVar
	{
		private string m_XMLName;

		private string m_RName;

		private string m_MName;

		private string m_PMName;

		private string m_PSName;

		private short m_ValueType;

		public string XMLName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_XMLName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_XMLName = value;
			}
		}

		public string RName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_RName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_RName = value;
			}
		}

		public string MName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_MName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_MName = value;
			}
		}

		public string PMName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_PMName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_PMName = value;
			}
		}

		public string PSName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_PSName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_PSName = value;
			}
		}

		public short ValueType
		{
			get
			{
				return m_ValueType;
			}
			set
			{
				m_ValueType = value;
			}
		}

		private void Class_Initialize_Renamed()
		{
			m_XMLName = "";
			m_RName = "";
			m_MName = "";
			m_PMName = "";
			m_PSName = "";
		}

		public TPSARVar()
		{
			Class_Initialize_Renamed();
		}

		public void SetVarName(string sXMLName, string sRPMName, string sStripperName)
		{
			m_XMLName = sXMLName;
			m_RName = sRPMName;
			m_MName = sRPMName;
			m_PMName = sRPMName;
			m_PSName = sStripperName;
		}

		public void SetAllVarName(string sXMLName, string sRName, string sMName, string sPMName, string sPSName)
		{
			m_XMLName = sXMLName;
			m_RName = sRName;
			m_MName = sMName;
			m_PMName = sPMName;
			m_PSName = sPSName;
		}

		public void SetRadFracVarName(string sXMLName, string sRName)
		{
			m_XMLName = sXMLName;
			m_RName = sRName;
		}

		public void SetMultiFracVarName(string sXMLName, string sMName)
		{
			m_XMLName = sXMLName;
			m_MName = sMName;
		}

		public string GetVarName(TPSARCls.TPSARHostEnum iTPSARHost)
		{
			string result = "";
			switch (iTPSARHost)
			{
			case TPSARCls.TPSARHostEnum.MultiFracTPSAR:
				result = m_MName;
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARHostEnum.PetroFracMainTPSAR:
				result = m_PMName;
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARHostEnum.PetroFracStripperTPSAR:
				result = m_PSName;
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARHostEnum.RadFracTPSAR:
				result = m_RName;
				break;
			case TPSARCls.TPSARHostEnum.BatchSepTPSAR:
				result = m_RName;
				break;
			default:
			{
				string text = "Unexpected TPSAR type encountered.";
				break;
			}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}

					case 5414:
						num = -1;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 2:
							text = Information.Err().Description + "  (Key = " + text2 + ").";
							break;
						default:
							goto end_IL_0001;
						}
						break;
					}
					if (Operators.CompareString(text, "", TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						result = text + "\r\nRoutine: InitTRVNames.";
					}
					break;
					end_IL_0001:;
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0001_dispatch = 5414;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[StandardModule]
